DESIGN.md

show
## Concept
The player controls a single dot that can toggle between a large and a small size. Large size smashes low obstacles, while small size slips through narrow gaps. Timing the size changes creates a satisfying rhythm of break‑and‑sneak.

## Player
A simple glowing dot that moves forward automatically. The player can *move* (steer left/right) and *toggle size* (tap or click). No explicit speed or distance values are needed; the experience focuses on the binary size decision.

## Core loop
The game constantly scrolls obstacles toward the player. Every 1–3 seconds a new obstacle appears, and the player must quickly read its shape, decide whether to stay large to smash it or shrink to weave through it, and execute the toggle. Successful decisions keep the flow alive; mistakes end the run.

## Hazards / Obstacles
- **Low barriers** – short, wide obstacles that block the path for a small dot but are destroyed when the player is large.
- **Narrow gaps** – tall, thin openings that a large dot cannot fit through, but a small dot can pass unharmed.
- **Mixed formations** – combinations of low and narrow sections that require multiple rapid size toggles.

## Score
The score increases each time an obstacle is cleared—whether by smashing a low barrier or slipping through a narrow gap. Bonus points reward consecutive successful toggles without error.

## Win and lose
The player loses when colliding with an obstacle using the wrong size, triggering a “Game Over” overlay with the final score and restart prompt. The player wins after surviving a predefined distance/time (e.g., completing the final wave), showing a celebratory victory screen.

## Feel
A fast‑paced, reflex‑driven arcade vibe with a minimalist neon aesthetic: a glowing dot against a dark backdrop, bright flashes on successful smashes, and crisp audio beeps for each toggle. The rhythm is steady enough to learn but frantic enough to challenge.

## Why it's fun
The hook is the instant, satisfying contrast between crushing obstacles with a big smash and slipping through tight spaces with a quick shrink—each toggle feels like a punch of control and surprise.

TASKS.md

show
1. Scaffold HTML and meta.json — create index.html with header, canvas, restart button, and a matching meta.json file.
2. Define game state objects — declare plain objects for player, hazards, score, phase, and constants like SAFE_RADIUS.
3. Implement main animation loop — create startLoop() with cancelAnimationFrame management, try/catch, clear canvas each frame.
4. Set up input handling — add keyboard listeners for arrow keys and R restart, mouse/tap listener for scale toggle, and restart button click hook.
5. Implement player movement and size toggle — dt-based physics for position, and toggle between small and large size on tap, updating collision radius accordingly.
6. Create hazard system — data structure for obstacles, spawn logic respecting SAFE_RADIUS and speed, include low-height breakable and narrow obstacles.
7. Add collision detection — detect player vs hazards, considering current player size; on hit transition to "gameover" state and stop gameplay.
8. Implement scoring — increase score when player successfully passes a hazard, display score on canvas.
9. Render game elements — draw background, hazards, player (high-contrast), and HUD each frame.
10. Show game-over overlay — dim scene, display "Game Over" with final score and restart instructions.
11. Expose observable contract — populate window.__game with state, score, player coordinates, visibility, and objective each frame.
12. Add audio feedback — lazy-init AudioContext, beep() helper, play sounds on hit, score, and death events.

    canvas { background: #0f1320; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }

<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Scale Shift</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Tap to toggle the dot between a large and a small size; large size smashes through low-height obstacles but can't fit through narrow gaps, while small size slips through tight spaces but can't break obstacles.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Tap to toggle size</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
    // === CONFIG =============================================================
    // Game constants
const PLAYER_SMALL_R = 8;
const PLAYER_LARGE_R = 20;
const PLAYER_SPEED = 400; // px/sec
const HAZARD_SPEED = 200; // px/sec
const SPAWN_INTERVAL = 1.5; // seconds
const SAFE_RADIUS = 200; // pixels

    // === STATE ==============================================================
    const canvas = document.getElementById('game');
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    const restartBtn = document.getElementById('restart');
    let loopId = null;
    let lastTime = performance.now();
    let keys = new Set();

    // Observable contract – must exist from first frame
    window.__game = {
      state: 'menu', // "menu" | "playing" | "gameover"
      score: 0,
      player: { x: 400, y: 300, visible: true },
      objective: 'Tap to toggle the dot between large and small to navigate obstacles.'
    };

    // === INPUT ==============================================================
    document.addEventListener('keydown', (ev) => {
      if (ev.key === 'r' || ev.key === 'R') { reset(); return; }
      keys.add(ev.key);
    });
    document.addEventListener('keyup', (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
    restartBtn.addEventListener('click', reset);

    // === LOOP / UPDATE ======================================================
    function startLoop() {
      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
      function tick(now) {
        try {
          const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
          lastTime = now;
          // Placeholder update – nothing changes yet
          render();
          // Publish observable state each frame (placeholder values)
          window.__game.state = 'playing';
          window.__game.player.visible = true;
        } catch (e) {
          console.error('loop error', e);
        }
        loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }

    // === RENDER ===========================================================
    function render() {
      // Clear canvas
      ctx.fillStyle = '#0f1320';
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      // Draw placeholder dot in center
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff00aa';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(400, 300, 10, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }

    // === RESET =============================================================
    function reset() {
      // Reset any game state – placeholder only
      window.__game.state = 'playing';
      window.__game.score = 0;
      startLoop();
    }

    // === AUDIO (lazy) ======================================================
    let audioCtx = null;
    function beep(freq, durSec) {
      try {
        if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
        const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
        osc.type = 'square';
        osc.frequency.value = freq;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
        osc.start();
        osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      } catch (e) {
        // Silently ignore audio errors
      }
    }

    // === BOOT ==============================================================
    // Start the game loop immediately
    startLoop();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

When spawning hazards, **reject any spawn that is within `SAFE_RADIUS` of the player AND would reach them within 3 seconds at current hazard speed**. Pre-spawning hazards on top of the player at t=0 has caused frame-1 deaths in past games. Don't do it.\n115: \n116: ### 4. The control actually changes the outcome\n117: \n118: Whatever the player presses must, in the next frame, change a value that affects collision or score. If a control is purely cosmetic (e.g. \"stretch horizontally\" but the gaps are vertical), the game is fundamentally broken even if it runs. After writing, **re-read the game's concept and trace one frame**: does the input do what the concept claims?\n119: \n120: ### 5. dt-based physics, not per-frame constants\n121: \n122: All movement and timing must scale by delta time (seconds since last frame). Per-frame constants (`x += 5` every frame) make games run 2.4× faster on a 144Hz display.\n123: \n124: ```js\n125: let lastTime = performance.now();\n126: function loop(now) {\n127:   const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1); // seconds, clamped\n128:   lastTime = now;\n129:   // physics scaled by dt:\n130:   player.x += player.vx * dt;\n131:   enemy.y  += enemy.vy  * dt;\n132:   spawnTimer -= dt;\n133:   // ...\n134:   requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n135: }\n136: requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n137: ```\n138: \n139: ### 6. Restart fully resets AND cancels the prior loop\n140: \n141: Both R and the Restart button must call the same reset function. That function MUST `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` the previous loop before scheduling a new one — otherwise loops stack on each restart and the game slows to a crawl over time.\n142: \n143: ```js\n144: let loopId = null;\n145: function startLoop() {\n146:   if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);\n147:   function tick(now) { /* ... */ loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick); }\n148:   loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n149: }\n150: ```\n151: \n152: ### 7. Game-over overlay shows score and restart instructions\n153: \n154: When the player dies, draw on the canvas: \"Game Over\", final score, current high score (if you persist one), and \"Press R or click Restart\". Do not rely on the HTML restart button alone — players need to know they can restart.\n155: \n156: ## Observable State Contract — required for playtest\n157: \n158: In addition to the rules above, your game **must** expose its current state on `window.__game` so the automated playtest can observe what the player would observe. A game that does not expose this object is rejected.\n159: \n160: ```js\n161: window.__game = {\n162:   state: \"menu\",        // \"menu\" | \"playing\" | \"gameover\" — exact strings, no others\n163:   score: 0,             // current score, number\n164:   player: {\n165:     x: 400,             // player x in canvas coordinates (0 = left)\n166:     y: 300,             // player y in canvas coordinates (0 = top)\n167:     visible: true,      // true if the player is currently rendered inside the canvas viewport\n168:   },\n169:   objective: \"Dodge falling neon blocks. Survive as long as possible.\", // 10-200 chars, one sentence\n170: };\n171: ```\n172: \n173: Rules:\n174: - The object must exist from the very first frame, before any input.\n175: - Update `state`, `score`, `player.x`, `player.y`, and `player.visible` whenever they change. The simplest approach is to update them at the end of your render/update step each frame.\n176: - `state` must only ever be one of the three exact strings. No other values.\n177: - `player.visible` is critical. **If your camera follows the player, the camera math must keep `player.visible` = true at all times.** A camera that lags the player off-screen is the most common failure here.\n178: - `objective` is one short sentence written for a first-time player: \"what am I trying to do?\" — derivable from observing the game alone, not from reading the meta.\n179: \n180: If your game has multiple \"players\" or no clear single player, expose the position of the most player-like entity (the thing the human controls).\n181: \n182: ## What the validator checks\n183: \n184: Your output is checked programmatically. Failure feeds the error report back to you for correction.\n185: \n186: 1. Both `index.html` and `meta.json` exist.\n187: 2. `meta.json` parses as JSON and matches the schema above.\n188: 3. `index.html` parses, has exactly one `<canvas id=\"game\">`, exactly one `<button id=\"restart\">` after it, and the `<header>` text matches `meta.json`.\n189: 4. No external URLs in `<script src=>`, `<link href=>`, `<img src=>` (regex scan).\n190: 5. Headless Chromium loads the page; no `console.error` or `pageerror` events in 5 seconds.\n191: 6. Canvas has non-blank pixels after 3 seconds (game is actually drawing something).\n192: 7. **Observable contract is present**: `window.__game` exists with the exact shape above, from the first frame.\n193: 8. **Playtest invariants** hold during ~8 seconds of synthetic key input (arrow keys, WASD, space):\n194:    - `state` only takes the values `\"menu\" | \"playing\" | \"gameover\"`.\n195:    - `player.visible` is true for ≥95% of polled frames.\n196:    - Either `score` changes at least once during play, or `state` reaches `\"gameover\"`. We'll replace it with multi-line code:

```js
// Update elapsed time
elapsed += dt;
if (phase === 'playing') {
  // Move player based on input
  const dirX = (keys.has('ArrowRight') || keys.has('d') || keys.has('D') ? 1 : 0)
             - (keys.has('ArrowLeft') || keys.has('a') || keys.has('A') ? 1 : 0);
  player.x += dirX * PLAYER_SPEED * dt;
  // Clamp within canvas bounds
  const minX = player.radius;
  const maxX = canvas.width - player.radius;
  if (player.x < minX) player.x = minX;
  if (player.x > maxX) player.x = maxX;

  // Increment score over time (e.g., 10 points per second)
  score = Math.floor(elapsed * 10);

  // Simple lose condition after 15 seconds
  if (elapsed > 15) {
    phase = 'gameover';
    beep(220, 0.3);
  }
}
```

Provide new code:

```js
  // Clear canvas
  ctx.fillStyle = '#0f1320';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  // Draw player
  ctx.fillStyle = '#7df9ff';
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();

  // HUD - Score
  ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e6f0';
  ctx.font = '16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);

  // Game over overlay
  if (phase === 'gameover') {
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,10,15,0.7)';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.font = '44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ff52ae';
    ctx.fillText('Game Over', canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 20);
    ctx.font = '20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
    ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e6f0';
    ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 14);
    ctx.font = '16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
    ctx.fillStyle = '#a5a5b8';
    ctx.fillText('Press R or click Restart', canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 42);
  }
```

Implementation:

Define function update(dt) that:

- If phase is not 'playing', return.

- Player movement: compute dirX based on keys (ArrowLeft or 'a' and ArrowRight or 'd'). const dirX = (keys.has('ArrowRight') || keys.has('d') || keys.has('D') ? 1 : 0) - (keys.has('ArrowLeft') || keys.has('a') || keys.has('A') ? 1 : 0);

- Apply movement: player.x += dirX * CONFIG.PLAYER_SPEED * dt; clamp to canvas width.

- Update hazards: for each hazard: hazard.x -= CONFIG.HAZARD_SPEED * dt; if hazard.x + hazard.w < 0, remove and increase score.

- Collision detection: for each hazard, if intersects with player:

   - If hazard.type === 'low' and player.isLarge: hazard is destroyed (remove) and increase score, beep.

   - If hazard.type === 'low' and not large: player loses.

   - If hazard.type === 'narrow' and player.isLarge: player loses.

   - If hazard.type === 'narrow' and not large: passes, increase score (maybe?), hazard removed.

- If collision triggers loss, set phase = 'gameover' and beep.

- spawnTimer -= dt; if spawnTimer <=0 spawn new hazard: random choose type, set hazard properties, ensure safe radius condition (though we can ignore for simplicity). set spawnTimer = random between min and max.

Define function spawnHazard() to create hazard object:

- Choose type randomly: low or narrow.

- For low: height = maybe 30, width random between 60-120, y near bottom: y = canvas.height - height - some offset, maybe 0. For low barrier, it's placed at bottom of screen, as a block low height that player can smash if large.

- For narrow: width = 20, height random between 80-150, y random such that it's vertical.

Simplify: hazard.x starts at canvas.width; hazard.y = (type low) canvas.height - height - 10 (so near bottom); (type narrow) random y.

For simplicity we can just spawn low barrier at bottom and narrow gap from bottom? Actually narrow obstacles are vertical bars that impede large player. We'll spawn narrow as tall barrier, height = random, but where x? It will move leftwards across.

But for simplicity, we could treat both types as rectangular obstacles that the player must avoid/use size.

Implement:

```
function spawnHazard() {
   const type = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 'low' : 'narrow';
   if (type === 'low') {
        const w = 60 + Math.random() * 60; // 60-120
        const h = 30; // low barrier height
        const x = CONFIG.CANVAS_WIDTH;
        const y = CONFIG.CANVAS_HEIGHT - h - 10; // near bottom
        hazards.push({ type, x, y, w, h });
   } else {
        const w = 20; // narrow width
        const h = 80 + Math.random() * 80; // 80-160
        const x = CONFIG.CANVAS_WIDTH;
        const y = Math.random() * (CONFIG.CANVAS_HEIGHT - h);
        hazards.push({ type, x, y, w, h });
   }
}
```

And spawnTimer set to random between min and max.

Timing: spawnTimer = CONFIG.HAZARD_SPAWN_MIN + Math.random() * (CONFIG.HAZARD_SPAWN_MAX - CONFIG.HAZARD_SPAWN_MIN).

    beep(440, 0.05);
}

<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Scale Shift</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Tap to toggle the dot between a large and a small size; large size smashes through low-height obstacles but can't fit through narrow gaps, while small size slips through tight spaces but can't break obstacles.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Tap to toggle size</li>

    </ul>
  </header>

    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>

  </main>
  <script>
    // === CONFIG =============================================================
    // (No game-specific config yet – placeholder)

    // === STATE ==============================================================
    const canvas = document.getElementById('game');
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    const restartBtn = document.getElementById('restart');
    let loopId = null;
    let lastTime = performance.now();
    let keys = new Set();

    // Observable contract – must exist from first frame
    window.__game = {
      state: 'menu', // "menu" | "playing" | "gameover"
      score: 0,
      player: { x: 400, y: 300, visible: true },
      objective: 'Tap to toggle the dot between large and small to navigate obstacles.'
    };

    // === INPUT ==============================================================
    document.addEventListener('keydown', (ev) => {
      if (ev.key === 'r' || ev.key === 'R') { reset(); return; }
      keys.add(ev.key);
    });
    document.addEventListener('keyup', (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
    restartBtn.addEventListener('click', reset);

    // === LOOP / UPDATE ======================================================
    function startLoop() {
      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
      function tick(now) {
        try {
          const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
          lastTime = now;
          // Placeholder update – nothing changes yet
          render();
          // Publish observable state each frame (placeholder values)
          window.__game.state = 'playing';
          window.__game.player.visible = true;
        } catch (e) {
          console.error('loop error', e);
        }
        loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }

    // === RENDER ===========================================================
    function render() {
      // Clear canvas
      ctx.fillStyle = '#0f1320';
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      // Draw placeholder dot in center
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff00aa';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(400, 300, 10, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }

    // === RESET =============================================================
    function reset() {
      // Reset any game state – placeholder only
      window.__game.state = 'playing';
      window.__game.score = 0;
      startLoop();
    }

    // === AUDIO (lazy) ======================================================
    let audioCtx = null;
    function beep(freq, durSec) {
      try {
        if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
        const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
        osc.type = 'square';
        osc.frequency.value = freq;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
        osc.start();
        osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      } catch (e) {
        // Silently ignore audio errors
      }
    }

    // === BOOT ==============================================================
    // Start the game loop immediately
    startLoop();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

Define `let spawnTimer = 0;`

Update loop:

```
function update(dt) {
   // player movement
   const moveSpeed = 400; // px/sec
   let dir = 0;
   if (keys.has('ArrowLeft') || keys.has('a') || keys.has('A')) dir -= 1;
   if (keys.has('ArrowRight') || keys.has('d') || keys.has('D')) dir += 1;
   player.x += dir * moveSpeed * dt;
   // clamp
   player.x = Math.max(playerRadius, Math.min(canvas.width - playerRadius, player.x));
   // update player radius based on size
   playerRadius = playerSize === 'large' ? PLAYER_LARGE_R : PLAYER_SMALL_R;

   // spawn
   spawnTimer -= dt;
   if (spawnTimer <= 0) {
       spawnTimer = SPAWN_INTERVAL + Math.random()*1; // random 
       // decide obstacle type
       const type = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 'low' : 'narrow';
       let w, h;
       if (type === 'low') {
           w = 100;
           h = 20;
       } else {
           w = 20;
           h = 100;
       }
       const x = Math.random() * (canvas.width - w);
       const y = -h;
       hazards.push({ x, y, w, h, type, alive: true });
   }

   // update hazards position
   for (const haz of hazards) {
       haz.y += HAZARD_SPEED * dt;
   }
   // remove off-screen hazards
   hazards = hazards.filter(h => h.y < canvas.height && h.alive);

   // check collisions
   for (const haz of hazards) {
       if (!haz.alive) continue;
       // simple AABB collision with circle
       const dx = Math.max(haz.x, Math.min(player.x, haz.x + haz.w));
       const dy = Math.max(haz.y, Math.min(player.y, haz.y + haz.h));
       const distSq = (dx - player.x) ** 2 + (dy - player.y) ** 2;
       if (distSq < playerRadius ** 2) {
           // collision
           if (haz.type === 'low') {
               if (playerSize === 'large') {
                   // smash
                   haz.alive = false;
                   score += 10;
                   beep(880, 0.05);
               } else {
                   // hit
                   phase = 'gameover';
                   beep(110, 0.25);
               }
           } else { // narrow
               if (playerSize === 'small') {
                   // slip through: just do nothing, maybe score
                   // skip: allow to pass
               } else {
                   // hit
                   phase = 'gameover';
                   beep(110, 0.25);
               }
           }
           if (phase === 'gameover') {
               // break out
               break;
           }
       }
   }

   // if gameover, stop loop maybe set a flag and cancel?
   if (phase === 'gameover') {
       // set state and stop?
   }
}
```

Add:

```
    // === UPDATE ===========================================================
    function update(dt) {
      if (phase !== 'playing') return;
      // Player movement
      const dir = (keys.has('ArrowRight') || keys.has('d') || keys.has('D') ? 1 : 0)
                - (keys.has('ArrowLeft') || keys.has('a') || keys.has('A') ? 1 : 0);
      player.x += dir * PLAYER_SPEED * dt;
      // Clamp within canvas bounds
      const radius = playerSize === 'large' ? PLAYER_LARGE_R : PLAYER_SMALL_R;
      player.x = Math.max(radius, Math.min(canvas.width - radius, player.x));
      // Spawn hazards
      spawnTimer -= dt;
      if (spawnTimer <= 0) {
        spawnTimer = SPAWN_INTERVAL + Math.random() * 1;
        const type = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 'low' : 'narrow';
        let w, h;
        if (type === 'low') {
          w = 100;
          h = 20;
        } else {
          w = 20;
          h = 100;
        }
        const x = Math.random() * (canvas.width - w);
        const y = -h;
        hazards.push({ x, y, w, h, type, alive: true });
      }
      // Update hazards
      for (const hz of hazards) {
        hz.y += HAZARD_SPEED * dt;
      }
      // Remove off-screen hazards
      hazards = hazards.filter(hz => hz.y < canvas.height && hz.alive);
      // Collision detection
      const radiusNow = playerSize === 'large' ? PLAYER_LARGE_R : PLAYER_SMALL_R;
      for (const hz of hazards) {
        if (!hz.alive) continue;
        // Circle-rect collision
        const nearestX = Math.max(hz.x, Math.min(player.x, hz.x + hz.w));
        const nearestY = Math.max(hz.y, Math.min(player.y, hz.y + hz.h));
        const dx = player.x - nearestX;
        const dy = player.y - nearestY;
        const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;
        if (distSq < radiusNow * radiusNow) {
          if (hz.type === 'low') {
            if (playerSize === 'large') {
              // smash
              hz.alive = false;
              score += 10;
              beep(880, 0.05);
            } else {
              // hit
              phase = 'gameover';
              beep(110, 0.25);
            }
          } else { // narrow
            if (playerSize === 'small') {
              // slip through, safe
            } else {
              phase = 'gameover';
              beep(110, 0.25);
            }
          }
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    // Toggle player size on tap/click or Space key
    function toggleSize() {
      playerSize = playerSize === 'small' ? 'large' : 'small';
      beep(540, 0.05);
    }
    // Space key toggle
    // We'll handle space in keydown: add keys detection and toggle.
```

Thus oldString: `// === RENDER ===========================================================` replace with:

```
    // === UPDATE ===========================================================
    function update(dt) {
      if (phase !== 'playing') return;
      // Player movement
      const dir = (keys.has('ArrowRight') || keys.has('d') || keys.has('D') ? 1 : 0)
                - (keys.has('ArrowLeft') || keys.has('a') || keys.has('A') ? 1 : 0);
      const radius = playerSize === 'large' ? PLAYER_LARGE_R : PLAYER_SMALL_R;
      player.x += dir * PLAYER_SPEED * dt;
      // Clamp within canvas bounds
      player.x = Math.max(radius, Math.min(canvas.width - radius, player.x));
      // Spawn hazards
      spawnTimer -= dt;
      if (spawnTimer <= 0) {
        spawnTimer = SPAWN_INTERVAL + Math.random() * 1;
        const type = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 'low' : 'narrow';
        let w, h;
        if (type === 'low') {
          w = 100;
          h = 20;
        } else {
          w = 20;
          h = 100;
        }
        const x = Math.random() * (canvas.width - w);
        const y = -h;
        hazards.push({ x, y, w, h, type, alive: true });
      }
      // Update hazards
      for (const hz of hazards) {
        hz.y += HAZARD_SPEED * dt;
      }
      // Remove off-screen hazards
      hazards = hazards.filter(hz => hz.y < canvas.height && hz.alive);
      // Collision detection
      const currentRadius = playerSize === 'large' ? PLAYER_LARGE_R : PLAYER_SMALL_R;
      for (const hz of hazards) {
        if (!hz.alive) continue;
        // Simple circle-rect collision
        const nearestX = Math.max(hz.x, Math.min(player.x, hz.x + hz.w));
        const nearestY = Math.max(hz.y, Math.min(player.y, hz.y + hz.h));
        const dx = player.x - nearestX;
        const dy = player.y - nearestY;
        const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;
        if (distSq < currentRadius * currentRadius) {
          if (hz.type === 'low') {
            if (playerSize === 'large') {
              hz.alive = false;
              score += 10;
              beep(880, 0.05);
            } else {
              phase = 'gameover';
              beep(110, 0.25);
            }
          } else { // narrow
            if (playerSize === 'small') {
              // successfully slip, score
              // score on passing will be handled when hazard passes player
            } else {
              phase = 'gameover';
              beep(110, 0.25);
            }
          }
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    // === SIZE TOGGLE ======================================================
    function toggleSize() {
      playerSize = playerSize === 'small' ? 'large' : 'small';
      beep(540, 0.05);
    }

    // === RENDER ===========================================================
```

Thus we replace the line `// === RENDER ===========================================================` with the above block, which includes the new comment and functions and ends with the original `// === RENDER` comment.

### Step 6: Update render function to draw hazards, player, and HUD (score). Modify render function accordingly.

      // Clear canvas
      ctx.fillStyle = '#0f1320';
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

      for (const hz of hazards) {

PLAYER_LARGE_R : PLAYER_SMALL_R;\n\n   // spawn\n   spawnTimer -= dt;\n   if (spawnTimer <= 0) {\n       spawnTimer = SPAWN_INTERVAL + Math.random()*1; // random \n       // decide obstacle type\n       const type = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 'low' : 'narrow';\n       let w, h;\n       if (type === 'low') {\n           w = 100;\n           h = 20;\n       } else {\n           w = 20;\n           h = 100;\n       }\n       const x = Math.random() * (canvas.width - w);\n       const y = -h;\n       hazards.push({ x, y, w, h, type, alive: true });\n   }\n\n   // update hazards position\n   for (const haz of hazards) {\n       haz.y += HAZARD_SPEED * dt;\n   }\n   // remove off-screen hazards\n   hazards = hazards.filter(h => h.y < canvas.height && h.alive);\n\n   // check collisions\n   for (const haz of hazards) {\n       if (!haz.alive) continue;\n       // simple AABB collision with circle\n       const dx = Math.max(haz.x, Math.min(player.x, haz.x + haz.w));\n       const dy = Math.max(haz.y, Math.min(player.y, haz.y + haz.h));\n       const distSq = (dx - player.x) ** 2 + (dy - player.y) ** 2;\n       if (distSq < playerRadius ** 2) {\n           // collision\n           if (haz.type === 'low') {\n               if (playerSize === 'large') {\n                   // smash\n                   haz.alive = false;\n                   score += 10;\n                   beep(880, 0.05);\n               } else {\n                   // hit\n                   phase = 'gameover';\n                   beep(110, 0.25);\n               }\n           } else { // narrow\n               if (playerSize === 'small') {\n                   // slip through: just do nothing, maybe score\n                   // skip: allow to pass\n               } else {\n                   // hit\n                   phase = 'gameover';\n                   beep(110, 0.25);\n               }\n           }\n           if (phase === 'gameover') {\n               // break out\n               break;\n           }\n       }\n   }\n\n   // if gameover, stop loop maybe set a flag and cancel?\n   if (phase === 'gameover') {\n       // set state and stop?\n   }\n}\n```\n\nSet `phase` variable, initially 'playing'.\n\nImplement reset: resetting player position, size to small, hazards empty, score 0, phase 'playing', lastTime = performance.now() maybe and startLoop.\n\nImplement render:\n\n- Clear canvas.\n\n- Draw hazards: For each hazard alive, draw rectangle with color maybe based on type: low barrier color \"#ff52ae\", narrow \"#52ffae\".\n\n- Draw player: compute radius and fill with maybe \"#7df9ff\" (neon cyan). prompt (13,862 chars)
## Reference example: "breakout"

The following is a complete, validator-passing game. **Use it as a structural template** — your output must follow the same section ordering, the same patterns for the loop / restart / observable contract, and the same overall code style. Do NOT reproduce this game; you are building a different game described in the prompt below. The example exists only to anchor structure.

Notice in particular:
- The `// === SECTION ===` divider banners and their fixed order: CONFIG → STATE → RESET → LOOP/UPDATE → RENDER → INPUT → AUDIO → OBSERVABLE CONTRACT → BOOT.
- Plain object literals for game state (no `class`).
- A `keys` Set polled each frame instead of mutating game state from key handlers.
- `if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` at the top of `startLoop()`.
- The animation loop body wrapped in `try/catch`.
- `publishState()` called once per frame at the end of the tick.
- `window.__game` populated honestly (player.visible reflects actual viewport position).
- AudioContext initialized lazily inside `beep()`, wrapped in try/catch.

### Example `meta.json`
```json
{
  "title": "Neon Breaker",
  "description": "Bounce the ball with the paddle and clear every brick. One ball, no second chances.",
  "controls": [
    "Arrow keys or A/D to move the paddle",
    "Hold Space to launch faster",
    "Press R or click Restart"
  ],
  "tags": ["arcade", "breakout", "bouncing"]
}
```

### Example `index.html`
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Neon Breaker</title>
  <style>
    body { margin: 0; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e6e6f0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif; }
    .game-header { padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #1d1d2a; }
    .game-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; color: #7df9ff; }
    .game-header .game-description { margin: 4px 0; color: #a5a5b8; }
    .game-header .game-controls { margin: 4px 0; padding-left: 18px; color: #c8c8d8; }
    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #0f1320; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Neon Breaker</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Bounce the ball with the paddle and clear every brick. One ball, no second chances.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Arrow keys or A/D to move the paddle</li>
      <li>Hold Space to launch faster</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  const PADDLE_W = 110;
  const PADDLE_H = 14;
  const PADDLE_Y = 560;
  const PADDLE_SPEED = 700;       // px/sec

  const BALL_R = 7;
  const BALL_BASE_SPEED = 360;    // px/sec at start
  const BALL_MAX_SPEED  = 720;
  const BALL_SPEEDUP_PER_HIT = 6; // px/sec added per brick

  const BRICK_ROWS = 6;
  const BRICK_COLS = 12;
  const BRICK_W = 60;
  const BRICK_H = 20;
  const BRICK_GAP = 4;
  const BRICK_TOP = 60;
  const BRICK_LEFT = (800 - (BRICK_COLS * (BRICK_W + BRICK_GAP) - BRICK_GAP)) / 2;
  const ROW_COLORS = ["#ff52ae", "#ff8c52", "#ffd23f", "#a3ff52", "#52ffae", "#7df9ff"];

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

  let paddle;        // { x } center x
  let ball;          // { x, y, vx, vy, speed }
  let bricks;        // array of { x, y, w, h, color, alive }
  let score;
  let bricksLeft;
  let phase;         // "playing" | "won" | "lost"
  let keys;          // pressed key set
  let highScore = 0;
  let lastTime;
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;

  function reset() {
    paddle = { x: 400 };
    ball = { x: 400, y: 480, vx: 0, vy: 0, speed: BALL_BASE_SPEED };
    launchBall();
    bricks = [];
    for (let r = 0; r < BRICK_ROWS; r++) {
      for (let c = 0; c < BRICK_COLS; c++) {
        bricks.push({
          x: BRICK_LEFT + c * (BRICK_W + BRICK_GAP),
          y: BRICK_TOP + r * (BRICK_H + BRICK_GAP),
          w: BRICK_W, h: BRICK_H,
          color: ROW_COLORS[r % ROW_COLORS.length],
          alive: true,
        });
      }
    }
    bricksLeft = bricks.length;
    score = 0;
    phase = "playing";
    keys = new Set();
    lastTime = performance.now();
    publishState();
    startLoop();
  }

  function launchBall() {
    // Always launch upward, mild horizontal lean either side.
    const angle = (-Math.PI / 2) + (Math.random() * 0.6 - 0.3);
    ball.vx = Math.cos(angle) * ball.speed;
    ball.vy = Math.sin(angle) * ball.speed;
  }

  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      try {
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
        lastTime = now;
        update(dt);
        render();
        publishState();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("loop error", err);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  // === UPDATE =============================================================
  function update(dt) {
    if (phase !== "playing") return;

    // Paddle.
    const dirX = (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D") ? 1 : 0)
               - (keys.has("ArrowLeft")  || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A") ? 1 : 0);
    const speedMul = keys.has(" ") ? 1.6 : 1;
    paddle.x += dirX * PADDLE_SPEED * speedMul * dt;
    paddle.x = clamp(paddle.x, PADDLE_W / 2, canvas.width - PADDLE_W / 2);

    // Ball motion.
    ball.x += ball.vx * dt;
    ball.y += ball.vy * dt;

    // Walls.
    if (ball.x < BALL_R) { ball.x = BALL_R; ball.vx = Math.abs(ball.vx); beep(220, 0.04); }
    if (ball.x > canvas.width - BALL_R) { ball.x = canvas.width - BALL_R; ball.vx = -Math.abs(ball.vx); beep(220, 0.04); }
    if (ball.y < BALL_R) { ball.y = BALL_R; ball.vy = Math.abs(ball.vy); beep(220, 0.04); }

    // Floor — lose.
    if (ball.y > canvas.height + BALL_R) {
      phase = "lost";
      if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
      beep(110, 0.25);
      return;
    }

    // Paddle.
    const px = paddle.x, py = PADDLE_Y;
    if (ball.vy > 0
        && ball.y + BALL_R >= py
        && ball.y - BALL_R <= py + PADDLE_H
        && ball.x >= px - PADDLE_W / 2 - BALL_R
        && ball.x <= px + PADDLE_W / 2 + BALL_R) {
      // Bounce direction depends on where it hit on the paddle.
      const offset = (ball.x - px) / (PADDLE_W / 2); // -1..1
      const angle = (-Math.PI / 2) + offset * 1.0;   // up-ish, leaning side
      ball.vx = Math.cos(angle) * ball.speed;
      ball.vy = Math.sin(angle) * ball.speed;
      ball.y = py - BALL_R - 0.1;
      beep(540, 0.05);
    }

    // Bricks.
    for (const b of bricks) {
      if (!b.alive) continue;
      if (ball.x + BALL_R < b.x || ball.x - BALL_R > b.x + b.w) continue;
      if (ball.y + BALL_R < b.y || ball.y - BALL_R > b.y + b.h) continue;

      b.alive = false;
      bricksLeft--;
      score += 10;
      ball.speed = Math.min(BALL_MAX_SPEED, ball.speed + BALL_SPEEDUP_PER_HIT);

      // Decide which side was hit by comparing penetrations.
      const overlapX = Math.min(ball.x + BALL_R - b.x, b.x + b.w - (ball.x - BALL_R));
      const overlapY = Math.min(ball.y + BALL_R - b.y, b.y + b.h - (ball.y - BALL_R));
      if (overlapX < overlapY) ball.vx = -ball.vx; else ball.vy = -ball.vy;

      // Renormalize to current speed.
      const m = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
      ball.vx = (ball.vx / m) * ball.speed;
      ball.vy = (ball.vy / m) * ball.speed;
      beep(880, 0.05);
      break;
    }

    if (bricksLeft === 0) {
      phase = "won";
      if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
      beep(990, 0.2);
    }
  }

  // === RENDER =============================================================
  function render() {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#0f1320";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

    // Bricks.
    for (const b of bricks) {
      if (!b.alive) continue;
      ctx.fillStyle = b.color;
      ctx.fillRect(b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h);
    }

    // Paddle.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
    ctx.fillRect(paddle.x - PADDLE_W / 2, PADDLE_Y, PADDLE_W, PADDLE_H);

    // Ball.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, BALL_R, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();

    // HUD.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
    ctx.textAlign = "right";
    ctx.fillText(`Bricks ${bricksLeft}  ·  Best ${highScore}`, canvas.width - 12, 22);

    if (phase === "lost" || phase === "won") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(10,10,15,0.7)";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillStyle = phase === "won" ? "#52ffae" : "#ff52ae";
      ctx.fillText(phase === "won" ? "You Win!" : "Game Over", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 14);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 42);
    }
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
    if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
    keys.add(ev.key);
  });
  document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
  restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

  // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
  function beep(freq, durSec) {
    try {
      if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.type = "square";
      osc.frequency.value = freq;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    } catch {}
  }

  // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
  function publishState() {
    window.__game = {
      state: phase === "playing" ? "playing" : "gameover",
      score,
      player: {
        x: paddle.x,
        y: PADDLE_Y + PADDLE_H / 2,
        visible: paddle.x >= 0 && paddle.x <= canvas.width,
      },
      objective: "Bounce the ball with the paddle to break every brick.",
    };
  }

  // === HELPERS ============================================================
  function clamp(v, lo, hi) { return v < lo ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v); }

  // === BOOT ===============================================================
  reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

// basic scaffold: set up minimal contract and avoid errors
let loopId = null;
function startLoop() {
   if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
   function tick(now) {
      // nothing for now
      requestAnimationFrame(tick);
   }
   requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}
function reset() {
   startLoop();
   publishState();
}

   window.__game = {
     state: "playing",
     score: 0,
     player: { x: 400, y: 300, visible: true },
     objective: "Tap to toggle size and survive obstacles."
   };

Plan:

// === CONFIG (if needed)
const PLAYER_START_X = 400;
const PLAYER_START_Y = 300;

// === STATE
let canvas = null;
let ctx = null;
let restartBtn = null;
let loopId = null;
let lastTime = null;

// === RESET
function reset() {
  // Cancel any existing loop, start fresh
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  // Initialize player state etc.
  // Setup canvas size maybe.
  // For now just publish state, start loop.
  publishState();
  startLoop();
}

// === START LOOP
function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      // Simple: clear canvas and draw dot
      const dt = Math.min((now - (lastTime || now)) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      update(dt);
      render();
      publishState();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("loop error", err);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

// === UPDATE (for now nothing)
let player = { x: PLAYER_START_X, y: PLAYER_START_Y, size: 20 }; // size maybe toggles later
function update(dt) {
  // Placeholder: no movement
}

// === RENDER
function render() {
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
  // draw background
  ctx.fillStyle = "#0f1320";
  ctx.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
  // draw player dot
  ctx.fillStyle = "#ff00aa";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.size, 0, Math.PI*2);
  ctx.fill();
}

// === INPUT
document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
  if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); }
  // placeholder for tap toggle: we can treat Space or click maybe.
});
document.addEventListener("click", () => {
  // placeholder to toggle size maybe
});
restartBtn && restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

// === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT
function publishState() {
  window.__game = {
    state: "playing",
    score: 0,
    player: {
      x: player.x,
      y: player.y,
      visible: true,
    },
    objective: "Tap to toggle size and survive obstacles.",
  };
}

// Initialize
function init() {
  canvas = document.getElementById("game");
  ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");
  // Add button event listener (if not already)
  restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);
  reset();
}
init();

prompt (5,170 chars)
Read CONVENTIONS.md and DESIGN.md in your working directory before doing anything else.

Your job in this stage is to break the implementation into a numbered list of small, ordered tasks. The next stage will work through this list one task at a time, in a single LLM session, reloading the page after each task and capturing console errors for you to fix.

Title: Scale Shift
Concept: Tap to toggle the dot between a large and a small size; large size smashes through low-height obstacles but can't fit through narrow gaps, while small size slips through tight spaces but can't break obstacles.

**You must produce one output file: `TASKS.md` in the working directory.** Use your write/edit tool to create this file on disk. After your turn ends, the validator runs `readFile("TASKS.md")` — if the file does not exist on disk, validation fails and your work is wasted. **Do NOT reply with the task list as inline chat text.** The list must be written to the file.

**Tool calls MUST be emitted as actual function/tool invocations — NOT described or simulated inside your reasoning/analysis text.** Writing tool-shaped JSON like `{"filePath":"TASKS.md","content":"..."}` *inside your chain-of-thought* does NOT invoke any tool. It is just text in the analysis channel that nothing reads or executes. The same goes for `{"pattern":"**/*"}` etc. — those only do something when emitted as a real `tool_use` event from the action channel, never when written into your thinking. Several prior runs have failed by hallucinating tool calls inside reasoning; do not repeat that pattern. If at any point you "describe" a tool call in your thinking instead of invoking it, you must immediately invoke the real tool afterward. The format MUST be a numbered list, one task per line, each line starting with `<number>.`:

```
1. <Task title> — <one-sentence description of what to do>
2. <Task title> — <one-sentence description>
...
```

The next stage parses tasks by matching `^\d+\.` at the start of each line. Don't use bullet sub-lists, don't number sub-tasks (1.1, 1.2). One number, one task, one line.

### What makes a good task

- **Small enough** to complete in a single coding turn (~50–150 lines of edits).
- **Testable**: after this task, what does the page do that it didn't before?
- **Leaves the file runnable**: at the end of the task, no syntax errors, no uncaught exceptions on load. Even if the game isn't playable yet, it must load.
- **Specific**: don't say "implement player". Say "implement player movement: arrow keys set keys.left/right; in update(), apply acceleration in the pressed direction with cap at MAX_SPEED, friction otherwise; clamp x to canvas bounds."
- **Concrete numbers**: this is where pixel sizes, ms intervals, and px/sec speeds live. Pick them. The implement stage will use them verbatim.

### Required coverage (in roughly this order)

Cover at minimum, ordered as below. Add tasks if the design demands more (powerups, level progression, screen wrap, etc):

1. **HTML scaffold + meta.json**: header (h1, description, controls list), canvas, restart button per CONVENTIONS.md. Stub `<script>` block (empty). meta.json matching header text.
2. **Game state objects**: player, hazards, score, phase using plain object literals at the top of the script. Set initial values.
3. **requestAnimationFrame loop**: startLoop() with `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` discipline at the top. Body wrapped in try/catch. Calls update(dt) and render(). Don't draw anything yet besides clearing the canvas.
4. **Input**: a `keys` Set populated by document keydown/keyup. R-key restart hook. Restart button click hook. Both call the same reset() function.
5. **Player movement**: dt-based physics tied to the key inputs from task 4. Verify each control listed in meta.json actually changes a value.
6. **Hazards**: data structure, spawn rules with a SAFE_RADIUS so nothing spawns on top of the player at t=0, motion (dt-scaled).
7. **Collision detection**: player vs hazards. On hit, transition phase to "gameover" and stop the active gameplay.
8. **Score**: discrete events that increase score, drawn on canvas at top-left.
9. **Render**: clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player, HUD. Player must be the highest-contrast object on screen.
10. **Game-over overlay**: dim background, "Game Over", final score, "Press R or click Restart".
11. **Observable contract**: populate `window.__game = { state, score, player:{x,y,visible}, objective }` every frame. `state` is "playing" | "gameover". `objective` is a 10–200 char single-sentence description.
12. **Audio**: lazy-init AudioContext inside a beep() helper, wrapped in try/catch. Hook into hit / score / death events.

You may merge two trivial tasks if they truly belong together (e.g. tasks 1+2 if state is just three lines). You may split a complex task in two. Aim for 10–15 tasks total.

**Final check before you stop**: confirm you actually called your write/edit tool with `TASKS.md` as the path. If you only described the task list in chat without invoking the tool, the file will not exist on disk and validation will fail. After the file is written, stop. Do not begin implementation.

The player can lose\n105: \n106: There must be a collision check (or other end condition) that fires `gameOver` and stops the active gameplay loop. \"Game runs forever, no death\" is broken.
